# Can I use AdCOM 1.0's code lists?

Generated by `npm run matrix`; don't edit it by hand. It grades every list of AdCOM 1.0 against every library of [`matrix/libraries.yaml`](../../../../matrix/libraries.yaml) on every dimension of [`matrix/method.yaml`](../../../../matrix/method.yaml), which is rendered in [`method.md`](method.md).

Every cell carries the label `static` and links the methodology beside the three things a reader checks it by. A cell holds what the library's own pinned evidence holds, and nothing a reader could not find there. Where that evidence does not answer the dimension's question, the cell says so in the dimension's `absent` words and links where the looking happened. It is never filled in from the language's defaults, from another library's declaration, or from what this repository takes the answer to be.

## The libraries

- `adcom-proto`, adcom-proto, proto, read from [its snapshot](https://github.com/IABTechLab/adcom-proto/tree/921f85e685fed869344437378f4dea2be19b7b49/proto/com/iabtechlab/adcom/v1/enums) at the pin `c46b47d117a0a572a18cd4418e54139b5f2363e6`. Its pinned file says of itself only "The following enumerations are referenced by attributes in AdCOM objects", and declares `syntax = "proto2"` with the package `com.iabtechlab.adcom.v1.enums` (proto/com/iabtechlab/adcom/v1/enums/enums.proto). It names no version of the specification, so none is recorded for it; the repository's README, which the snapshot doesn't pin, titles the work as in progress. Its own report is [`adcom-proto.md`](adcom-proto.md).
- `jsonspecs-proto`, jsonspecs proto, proto, read from [its target](../../targets/proto) at the pin `291e279be5b72a54b986f7e1a757fbb4175ff097`. Its own files say "Generated by `npm run proto` from this published JSON Schema; don't edit it by hand" and name the schema document each one comes from (specs/adcom/targets/proto/com/jsonspecshq/adcom/v1/lists.proto). It claims no version of its own and nothing official: this repository's target rules say of every target that no specification's owner publishes it. Its own report is [`jsonspecs-proto.md`](jsonspecs-proto.md).
- `prebid-openrtb`, Prebid adcom1, go, read from [its snapshot](https://github.com/prebid/openrtb/tree/0d01af1cd27503d7edd0584fadb7e740ab83cc9c/adcom1) at the pin `1591f1764b7f66df16c190d5b383927c6e376a3c`. "Package adcom1 provides AdCOM 1.0 types" (adcom1/adcom1.go), and the README in its pinned folder calls it "AdCOM 1.0 types for Go programming language". It is the one row that names the version of the specification it carries, and it claims types rather than validation: nothing in the pinned folder says it checks a payload against the specification. Its own report is [`prebid-openrtb.md`](prebid-openrtb.md).
